## Ibn Karram - Founder of the Karramiyya sect
me_karrami_01 = { 
	name = "Muhammad" 
	dynasty = me_karrami_dynasty
	rite = "karrami"
	culture = "persian"
	trait = education_learning_4
	trait = heresiarch
	trait = gregarious
	trait = zealous
	trait = ME_missionary

	father = me_karrami_02
	806.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	866.1.1 = {
		effect = {
			add_trait = hafidh
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = hafidh
				value = 100
			}
			learn_language_of_culture = culture:bedouin
		}
	}
	868.1.1 = {
		death = yes
	}
	868.2.1 = {
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: Ibn Karram: died in Jerusalem and was buried by the Jericho Gate; registered at Jaffa, the nearest free province (a province holds one saint)
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = {
				PROVINCE = province:5962
				FAITH = karrami
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = karrami }
		}
	}
}

## The Hashimids
3390011 = {
	name = "Hamza" # Hamza ibn Abdul-Mutalib, paternal uncle of Prophet Muhammad
	dynasty_house = house_me_banu_hashim
	sexuality = heterosexual
	rite = tabiun
	culture = "bedouin"
	trait = quraysh
	trait = education_martial_4
	trait = physique_good_3
	trait = zealous

	father = 33924 # Abdul Muttalib, the Prophet's grandfather (exists in bedouin.txt)
	568.2.28 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	625.3.22 = {
		death = { death_reason = death_me_hamza }
	}
	625.4.22 = {
		# ME: Hamza: the Prophet's uncle, martyr of Uhud
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: buried at Uhud, outside Medina; registered at al-Safra in Medina county (a province holds one saint)
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = {
				PROVINCE = province:6219
				FAITH = sunni
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = sunni }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = masmudi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ithna_ashari }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = alevi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ghulat }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ishaqiya }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = shia }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = zayidi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ibadi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = kharijite }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = najdat }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = sufri }
		}
	}
}

me_hashimid_002 = {
	name = "al-Abbas" # al-Abbas ibn Ali, son of Ali and Umm al-Banin Fatima bint Hizam al-Kilabiyya; standard-bearer of his brother Husayn at Karbala
	dynasty_house = house_me_banu_hashim
	rite = tabiun
	culture = "bedouin"
	trait = quraysh
	trait = brave
	trait = loyal
	trait = compassionate

	father = 33911 # Ali ibn Abi Talib (exists in bedouin.txt)
	647.5.15 = { # 4 Sha'ban 26 AH
		birth = yes
	}
	680.10.10 = { # 10 Muharram 61 AH, at Karbala
		death = { death_reason = death_me_abbas_ibn_ali }
	}
	680.11.10 = {
		# ME: laid to rest at Karbala, beside his brother Imam Husayn
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: his own shrine stands at Karbala; registered at Sarsar, the nearest free province (a province holds one saint)
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = { # ME: no awliya for Sadr al-Islam itself
				PROVINCE = province:4827
				FAITH = ithna_ashari
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ithna_ashari }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = alevi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ghulat }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ishaqiya }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = shia }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = zayidi }
		}
	}
}

## Umayyads

sufyani002 = { # al-Farah bint Abu Sufyan
	name = "al-Farah"
	female = yes
	dynasty = 597 # Umayyad
	rite = "shafii"
	culture = "bedouin"

	father = 163028 # Abu Sufyan ibn Harb (exists in bedouin.txt)
	595.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	620.1.1 = {
		add_spouse = asadid023 # Abd 'Abu Ahmad' ibn Jahsh al-Asadi
	}
	660.1.1 = { 
		death = yes
	}
}
sufyani001 = { # Ramlah bint Abu Sufyan, wife of Prophet Muhammad
	name = "Ramlah"
	female = yes
	dynasty = 597 # Umayyad
	rite = tabiun 
	culture = "bedouin"
	trait = quraysh
	trait = zealous
	trait = chaste

	father = 163028 # Abu Sufyan ibn Harb (exists in bedouin.txt)
	589.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	611.1.1 = {
		add_spouse = asadid022 # Ubaydullah ibn Jahsh al-Asadi, early Islamic apostate
	}
	614.1.1 = {
		remove_spouse = asadid022 # They seperate after his apostosy
	}
	628.1.1 = {
		add_spouse = 33922 #Prophet Muhammad
	}
	664.7.1 = { 
		death = yes
	}
	664.8.1 = {
		# ME: laid to rest in Jannat al-Baqi', Medina
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: buried in al-Baqi', Medina; registered at al-Rabadha, the nearest free province (a province holds one saint)
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = { # ME: no awliya for Sadr al-Islam itself
				PROVINCE = province:6214
				FAITH = sunni
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = sunni }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = masmudi }
		}
	}
}

## Banu Asad ibn Khuzaymah
asadid001 = {
	name = "Habib" # Habib ibn Madhahir, Companion of Prophet Muhammad, Ali ibn Abi Talib, and al-Husayn, martyred in the Battle of Karbala
	dynasty = me_banu_asad
	rite = tabiun
	culture = "bedouin"
	trait = zealous
	trait = brave
	trait = strong

	father = asadid002
	605.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	680.10.10 = {
	   death = {
		death_reason = death_karbala
		}
	}
	680.11.10 = {
		# ME: Habib ibn Madhahir: martyr of Karbala
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: buried in the shrine of Imam Husayn at Karbala; registered at Ayn al-Tamr, the nearest free province (a province holds one saint)
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = { # ME: no awliya for Sadr al-Islam itself
				PROVINCE = province:5990
				FAITH = ithna_ashari
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ithna_ashari }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = alevi }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ghulat }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = ishaqiya }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = shia }
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = zayidi }
		}
	}
}

		}

## Qarmatians
qarm001 = { # https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_Sa%27id_al-Jannabi
	name = "Hasan" # Abu Sa'id Hasan ibn Bahram al-Jannabi
	dynasty = 12279
	rite = "ismaili" # Hamdan Qarmat didn't begin altering Isma'ili doctrines until 899
	culture = "persian" # He is from Jannaba (Bandar Ganaveh), which is is in Fars.

	845.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	895.1.1 = {
		add_spouse = qarm003
		effect = {
			learn_language_of_culture = culture:bedouin
		}
	}
	899.1.1 = {
		rite = "qarmatian" # Hamdan Qarmat didn't begin altering Isma'ili doctrines until 899
	}
	913.1.1 = {
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder
		}
	}
	913.2.1 = {
		# ME: Abu Sa'id al-Jannabi: founder of the Qarmatian state of Bahrayn
		effect_even_if_dead = {
			# ME: murdered at al-Ahsa, the seat of the Qarmatian state, in 913
			me_canonize_early_muslim_effect = {
				PROVINCE = province:6154
				FAITH = qarmatian
			}
			me_wali_revered_by_effect = { FAITH = qarmatian }
		}
	}
}
